Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any multiple module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 533. The projective resolution has length at most 441 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the multiple space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 533 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 441. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a multiple algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 999. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 191/2.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
